 283: const Store = {
 284:   key:'kidmath_v1',
 285:   data:null,
 286:   defaults(){
 287:     const sk = () => ({lvl:1, up:0, down:0, wins:0, tries:0});
 288:     return {v:1, stars:0, diff:'auto', voiceURI:null, rot:{}, irot:{}, seen:{}, calm:false, keepAlive:true,
 289:       skills:{count:sk(), numeral:sk(), produce:sk(), flash:sk(), compare:sk(), compose:sk(), add:sk()}};
 290:   },
 291:   load(){
 292:     let d = null;
 293:     try{ d = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(this.key) || 'null'); }catch(e){ d = null; }
 294:     const base = this.defaults();
 295:     if(d && d.v === 1 && d.skills){
 296:       for(const k in base.skills){ base.skills[k] = Object.assign(base.skills[k], d.skills[k] || {}); }
 297:       base.stars = (typeof d.stars === 'number' && d.stars >= 0) ? d.stars : 0;
 298:       base.diff = ['auto','1','2','3'].includes(d.diff) ? d.diff : 'auto';
 299:       base.voiceURI = (typeof d.voiceURI === 'string') ? d.voiceURI : null;
 300:       // ç»“æž„æ ¡éªŒï¼ˆP2-02ï¼‰: rot/irot ä»…æŽ¥å—æ•°å€¼è¡¨, seen ä»…æŽ¥å—å¸ƒå°”çœŸå€¼è¡¨
 301:       const numMap = o => { const r = {};
 302:         if(o && typeof o === 'object' && !Array.isArray(o)) for(const k in o){ if(typeof o[k] === 'number' && isFinite(o[k])) r[k] = o[k]; }
 303:         return r; };
 304:       base.rot = numMap(d.rot); base.irot = numMap(d.irot);
 305:       const seen = {};
 306:       if(d.seen && typeof d.seen === 'object' && !Array.isArray(d.seen)) for(const k in d.seen){ if(d.seen[k] === true) seen[k] = true; }
 307:       base.seen = seen;
 308:       base.calm = d.calm === true;
 309:       base.keepAlive = d.keepAlive !== false;
 310:     }
 311:     this.data = base;
 312:   },

 314:   reset(){ this.data = this.defaults(); this.save(); }

 358:   pickVoice(){
 359:     const stored = Store.data && Store.data.voiceURI;
 360:     const zh = this.voices.filter(v => /^zh/i.test(v.lang) || /ä¸­æ–‡|Chinese/i.test(v.name));
 361:     if(stored){ const m = this.voices.find(v => v.voiceURI === stored); if(m){ this.voice = m; return; } }
 362:     const score = v => (/zh[-_]CN/i.test(v.lang) ? 4 : 0)
 363:       + (/Tingting|Ting-Ting|å©·å©·/i.test(v.name) ? 3 : 0)
 364:       + (/Xiaoxiao|æ™“æ™“/i.test(v.name) ? 3 : 0)
 365:       + (v.localService ? 1 : 0) + (v.default ? .5 : 0);
 366:     this.voice = zh.sort((a,b) => score(b) - score(a))[0] || null;
 367:   },

 369:   unlock(text){

 376:   /* æŒ‡ä»¤/åé¦ˆé€šé“: æœ€æ–°è¦†ç›– + å®‰å…¨æ‰“æ–­(cancel åŽ â‰¥180ms) */
 377:   say(text, opts){
 378:     opts = opts || {};
 379:     if(this.noTTS || !this.unlocked || !text) return Promise.resolve();
 380:     if(opts.polite){
 381:       try{ if(speechSynthesis.speaking || speechSynthesis.pending) return Promise.resolve(); }catch(e){}
 382:       if(Date.now() - this.lastReq < 1500) return Promise.resolve();
 383:     }
 384:     this.lastReq = Date.now();
 385:     try{ speechSynthesis.resume(); }catch(e){}   // å†›è§„4
 386:     let busy = false;
 387:     try{ busy = speechSynthesis.speaking || speechSynthesis.pending; }catch(e){}
 388:     if(busy || this._cancelT){
 389:       return new Promise(res => {
 390:         if(this._pend) this._pend.res();          // å†›è§„8: æ—§çš„è¢«æœ€æ–°è¦†ç›–ï¼Œç­‰å¾…è€…ç«‹å³æ”¾è¡Œ
 391:         this._pend = {text, opts, res};
 392:         if(!this._cancelT){

 394:           this._instrActive = false;
 395:           this._countQ.length = 0; this._latestCount = null;
 396:           this._cancelT = setTimeout(() => {      // å†›è§„2: cancel åŽ â‰¥150ms
 397:             this._cancelT = null;
 398:             const p = this._pend; this._pend = null;
 399:             if(p) this._speakNow(p.text, p.opts, p.res);
 400:           }, 180);
 401:         }
 402:       });
 403:     }
 404:     return new Promise(res => this._speakNow(text, opts, res));
 405:   },
 406: 
 407:   politeSay(text){ return this.say(text, {polite:true}); },
 408: 
 409:   _mkUtter(text, opts){
 410:     const u = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
 411:     if(this.voice) u.voice = this.voice;
 412:     u.lang = 'zh-CN';                              // æ’è®¾ langï¼ˆæ—  voice æ—¶å…œåº•ï¼‰
 413:     u.rate = opts.rate || .92; u.pitch = opts.pitch || 1.1; u.volume = 1;
 414:     this._refs.push(u);                            // å†›è§„6: é˜² GC
 415:     return u;
 416:   },
 417:   _release(u){ const i = this._refs.indexOf(u); if(i >= 0) this._refs.splice(i, 1); },
 418:   _markWork(){ this.everWorked = true; UI.hideNoSound(); },
 419: 
 420:   _speakNow(text, opts, res){
 421:     let u;
 422:     try{ u = this._mkUtter(text, opts); }catch(e){ res(); return; }
 423:     this.log.push({t:Date.now(), text});
 424:     const gen = this._gen;
 425:     let settled = false, started = false;
 426:     this._instrActive = !opts.isCount;
 427:     if(!opts.isCount) Sfx.duck(true);              // è¯­éŸ³æœŸé—´éŸ³æ•ˆé—ªé¿ï¼ˆU7ï¼‰
 428:     const finish = () => { if(!settled){ settled = true; if(!opts.isCount){ this._instrActive = false; Sfx.duck(false); } this._release(u); res(); } };
 429:     u.onstart = () => { started = true; this._markWork(); };
 430:     u.onboundary = () => { started = true; this._markWork(); };
 431:     u.onend = () => { this._markWork(); finish(); };

 587:   _silentWav(){
 588:     const sr = 8000, n = 4000, size = 44 + n, b = new Uint8Array(size), dv = new DataView(b.buffer);
 589:     const ws = (o, s) => { for(let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) b[o + i] = s.charCodeAt(i); };
 590:     ws(0,'RIFF'); dv.setUint32(4, 36 + n, true); ws(8,'WAVE'); ws(12,'fmt ');
 591:     dv.setUint32(16,16,true); dv.setUint16(20,1,true); dv.setUint16(22,1,true);
 592:     dv.setUint32(24,sr,true); dv.setUint32(28,sr,true); dv.setUint16(32,1,true); dv.setUint16(34,8,true);
 593:     ws(36,'data'); dv.setUint32(40,n,true); b.fill(128,44);
 594:     let s = ''; for(let i = 0; i < size; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(b[i]);
 595:     return 'data:audio/wav;base64,' + btoa(s);
 596:   },
 597:   tone(f0, f1, dur, type, vol, when){
 598:     try{

 607:       o.connect(g); g.connect(this.master); o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + .06);
 608:     }catch(e){}
 609:   },

 672:       case 'compose':{
 673:         // L3 ä¸Šé™ 8: ä¿è¯ 88px æºç‰©å“åœ¨ç«–å±ä¹Ÿæ”¾å¾—ä¸‹ï¼ˆP1-04ï¼‰
 674:         const total = L === 1 ? 3 + ri(2) : L === 2 ? 5 : 6 + ri(3);
 675:         const a = 1 + ri(total - 1);
 676:         return {a, b: total - a};
 677:       }
 678:       case 'add':{
 679:         const total = L === 1 ? 2 + ri(3) : L === 2 ? 3 + ri(3) : 6 + ri(5);
 680:         let a = 1 + ri(Math.min(5, total - 1)); let b = total - a;
 681:         if(b > 5){ b = 5; a = total - b; }
 682:         return {a, b};
 683:       }
 684:     }
 685:   }
 686: };

 688: /* ============ 7. UI æ‚é¡¹ ============ */
 689: const UI = {

 695:   },

 722: /* ============ 8. æ´»åŠ¨é€šç”¨ä»¶ ============ */
 723: function scatterPositions(n){
 724:   const cols = n <= 3 ? n : n <= 6 ? 3 : n <= 8 ? 4 : 4;
 725:   const rows = Math.ceil(n / cols);
 726:   const cells = [];
 727:   for(let r = 0; r < rows; r++) for(let c = 0; c < cols; c++) cells.push({r, c});
 728:   return shuffle(cells).slice(0, n).map(({r, c}) => ({
 729:     x: (c + .5) / cols * 100 + (rng() * 8 - 4),
 730:     y: (r + .5) / rows * 100 + (rng() * 8 - 4)
 731:   }));
 732: }
 733: function distractors(correct, max){
 734:   const set = new Set([correct]);
 735:   const cand = shuffle([correct - 1, correct + 1, correct - 2, correct + 2, correct + 3]
 736:     .filter(v => v >= 1 && v <= Math.max(max, correct + 2) && v <= 10));
 737:   for(const v of cand){ if(set.size < 3) set.add(v); }
 738:   let guard = 20;
 739:   while(set.size < 3 && guard--){ const v = 1 + ri(10); set.add(v); }
 740:   return shuffle([...set]);
 741: }
 742: function hostBox(stage, charId, opt){
 743:   const b = el('div','hostbox', stage);
 744:   imgTag(charImg(charId), 'host', b);
 745:   const nm = el('div','hname', b); nm.textContent = CHARS[charId];
 746:   if(opt && opt.small) b.style.width = '22vmin';
 747:   return b;
 748: }
 749: async function sayAs(box, text, opts){
 750:   if(box) box.classList.add('talking');
 751:   await Speech.say(text, opts);
 752:   if(box) box.classList.remove('talking');
 753: }
 754: /* ä¾æ¬¡ç¤ºèŒƒç‚¹æ•°ï¼ˆç­”é”™åŽçš„è„šæ‰‹æž¶ï¼‰ã€‚é«˜äº®åŠ åœ¨å†…å±‚ img ä¸Šâ€”â€”img æ— åŸºç¡€ transform, åŠ¨ç”»ä¸ä¼šä½¿å…ƒç´ è·³ä½ */
 755: async function demoCount(ctx, items){
 756:   for(let i = 0; i < items.length; i++){
 757:     if(!ctx.alive()) return;
 758:     const t = items[i].querySelector('img') || items[i];
 759:     t.classList.remove('hl'); void t.offsetWidth;
 760:     t.classList.add('hl'); Sfx.pop();
 761:     await Speech.say(NUM_CN[i + 1], {tmo:1500, rate:1.0});
 762:     await wait(140);
 763:   }
 764: }
 765: /* äº”æ ¼æ¡†/éª°å­å¼ç¨³å®šç‚¹ä½ï¼ˆæ„Ÿæ•°ä¸Žç‚¹é˜µæç¤ºç”¨, V4ï¼‰ */
 766: function frameSpots(n){
 767:   const P = {
 768:     1:[[50,50]], 2:[[32,50],[68,50]], 3:[[26,64],[50,36],[74,64]],
 769:     4:[[32,32],[68,32],[32,68],[68,68]], 5:[[28,30],[72,30],[50,50],[28,70],[72,70]]
 770:   };
 771:   return P[Math.min(n,5)] || P[5];
 772: }
 773: /* å¹½çµæ‰‹ç¤ºèŒƒï¼ˆP0-U1/U3ï¼‰: é¦–é‡æ´»åŠ¨ç±»åž‹æ—¶æ¼”ç¤ºæ‰‹åŠ¿, å­©å­ä»»æ„ç‚¹å‡»å³è·³è¿‡ */
 774: const Ghost = {
 775:   el:null,
 776:   async run(ctx, anchors){
 777:     if(!anchors || !anchors.length) return;
 778:     if(!this.el){ this.el = el('div','ghosthand', document.body); this.el.textContent = 'ðŸ‘†'; }
 779:     const g = this.el;
 780:     let skipped = false;
 781:     const skip = () => { skipped = true; };
 782:     document.addEventListener('pointerdown', skip, {once:true});
 783:     const r0 = anchors[0].getBoundingClientRect();
 784:     g.style.transition = 'none'; g.style.display = 'block';
 785:     g.style.left = (r0.left + r0.width * .5) + 'px'; g.style.top = (r0.top + r0.height + 40) + 'px';
 786:     void g.offsetWidth; g.style.transition = '';
 787:     for(const a of anchors){
 788:       if(skipped || !ctx.alive()) break;
 789:       const r = a.getBoundingClientRect();
 790:       g.style.left = (r.left + r.width * .45) + 'px';
 791:       g.style.top = (r.top + r.height * .55) + 'px';
 792:       await wait(650);
 793:       if(skipped || !ctx.alive()) break;
 794:       g.style.transform = 'scale(.76)';
 795:       const t = a.querySelector('img') || a;
 796:       t.classList.remove('hl'); void t.offsetWidth; t.classList.add('hl');
 797:       Sfx.pop();
 798:       await wait(260);
 799:       g.style.transform = '';
 800:       await wait(220);
 801:     }
 802:     g.style.display = 'none';
 803:     document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', skip);
 804:   }
 805: };
 806: /* æ•°å­—ç‰Œé—®ç­”: resolve {firstTry}
 807:    è„šæ‰‹æž¶é˜¶æ¢¯(T5): é”™1æ¬¡â†’ç¤ºèŒƒé‡æ•°+å†é—®; é”™2æ¬¡â†’ç¤ºèŒƒåŽæ­£ç¡®ç‰Œè„‰å†²æç¤º(æ­¤åŽå®Œæˆä¸€å¾‹è®¡ hinted) */
 808: function askNumber(stage, ctx, cfg){
 809:   return new Promise(resolve => {
 810:     let firstTry = true, wrongs = 0, lock = false;
 811:     const bar = el('div','cards', stage);
 812:     stage.dataset.answer = cfg.correct;
 813:     for(const v of distractors(cfg.correct, cfg.max || 10)){
 814:       const c = el('button','ncard pop-in', bar);
 815:       c.textContent = v; c.dataset.value = v;
 816:       c.addEventListener('pointerdown', async e => {
 817:         if(e.isPrimary === false || lock || !ctx.alive()) return;
 818:         lock = true; Sfx.ensure(); UI.bump(c);
 819:         if(v === cfg.correct){
 820:           c.classList.add('right'); Sfx.ding();
 821:           UI.confetti(stage, 18);
 822:           await sayAs(cfg.hostEl, `${NUM_CN[v]}ï¼${pick(PRAISES)}${cfg.doneLine}`);
 823:           if(!ctx.alive()) return;
 824:           bar.remove();
 825:           resolve({firstTry});
 826:         }else{
 827:           firstTry = false; wrongs++; Sfx.soft(); c.classList.add('dim');
 828:           await sayAs(cfg.hostEl, `è¿™æ˜¯${NUM_CN[v]}ã€‚æˆ‘ä»¬å†ä¸€èµ·æ•°ä¸€æ•°ï¼`);
 829:           if(!ctx.alive()) return;
 830:           bar.style.visibility = 'hidden';
 831:           if(cfg.demo) await cfg.demo();
 832:           if(!ctx.alive()) return;
 833:           bar.style.visibility = '';
 834:           if(wrongs >= 2){
 835:             const rightCard = bar.querySelector(`[data-value="${cfg.correct}"]`);
 836:             if(rightCard){ rightCard.style.animation = 'pulse 1s ease-in-out infinite'; }
 837:           }
 838:           await sayAs(cfg.hostEl, cfg.reask);
 839:           App.setInstruction(cfg.reask);
 840:           lock = false;
 841:         }
 842:       });
 843:     }
 844:   });
 845: }

 931:   async numeral(stage, ctx){
 932:     const {target, range, cnt} = ctx.params;
 933:     const host = hostBox(stage, ctx.host, {small:true});
 934:     // ç‚¹é˜µæç¤ºå¡: æ•°ä¸€æ•°ç‚¹ç‚¹, æ‰¾åˆ°å¯¹åº”æ•°å­—ï¼ˆæ•°é‡â†’ç¬¦å·é“¾æŽ¥ï¼‰
 935:     const tc = el('div','target-card', host);

 937:     for(let i = 0; i < target; i++) el('i','', dots);
 938:     const field = el('div','field', stage);

 940:     let guard = 40;
 941:     while(vals.length < cnt && guard--){ const v = 1 + ri(range); if(!vals.includes(v)) vals.push(v); }

 944:     const intro = `æ•°ä¸€æ•°å¡ç‰‡ä¸Šçš„ç‚¹ç‚¹ï¼Œæ‰¾ä¸€æ‰¾ï¼Œæ•°å­—${NUM_CN[target]}è—åœ¨å“ªä¸ªæ°”çƒé‡Œï¼Ÿ`;

 978:      è‡ªç”±å–‚(å¯å¤šå¯å°‘), å­©å­è‡ªå·±æŒ‰ç»¿å‹¾æäº¤; åªæœ‰é¦–æ¬¡æäº¤æ°å¥½ N è®¡ independentã€‚

 980:   async produce(stage, ctx){

 988:     if(L >= 3) dots.style.display = 'none';        // æŒ‘æˆ˜æ¡£: åªç»™æ•°å­—ç¬¦å·, ä¸ç»™ç‚¹é˜µè„šæ‰‹æž¶

1001:     const intro = `æˆ‘æ˜¯${CHARS[ctx.host]}ï¼æˆ‘æƒ³åƒ${qtyCN(n)}${it.cl}${it.n}ã€‚å–‚å¥½äº†ï¼Œå°±æŒ‰ç»¿è‰²çš„å‹¾å‹¾å‘Šè¯‰æˆ‘å“¦ï¼`;

1263:     this.host = world.chars[rot[world.id] % world.chars.length];

1265:                  world.chars[(rot[world.id] + 1) % world.chars.length]];

1272:   alive(){ return this.sid === App.sessionId && App.currentCtx === this; }
1273:   after(ms, fn){ const id = setTimeout(() => { this.timers.delete(id); if(this.alive()) fn(); }, T(ms)); this.timers.add(id); return id; }
1274:   touch(){ this.lastTouch = Date.now(); }
1275:   /* é¦–é‡æ­¤æ´»åŠ¨ç±»åž‹ â†’ å¹½çµæ‰‹ç¤ºèŒƒï¼ˆå¯è¢«å­©å­ä»»æ„ç‚¹å‡»è·³è¿‡, ä¸é˜»å¡žè¾“å…¥ï¼‰ */
1276:   maybeDemo(anchors){
1277:     if(QP.get('demo') === '0') return;
1278:     if(Store.data.seen[this.type]) return;
1279:     Store.data.seen[this.type] = true; Store.save();

1281:     const self = this;

1283:   }
1284:   nudge(text, demoAnchor){
1285:     // è½»å£°æé†’: 9s æ— æ“ä½œä¸”å¼•æ“Žç©ºé—²æ—¶é‡å¤å½“å‰æŒ‡ä»¤, æœ€å¤š2æ¬¡; ç¬¬2æ¬¡é™„å¸¦å¹½çµæ‰‹æŒ‡ç‚¹ï¼ˆU3/U8ï¼‰
1286:     this._nudgeText = text; this._nudgeAnchor = demoAnchor || null;
1287:     if(this._nudgeTimer) return;

1289:       if(!this.alive()){ clearInterval(iv); return; }
1290:       if(this.nudges >= 2) { clearInterval(iv); return; }
1291:       if(Date.now() - this.lastTouch > T(9000)){
1292:         this.nudges++; this.lastTouch = Date.now();
1293:         Speech.politeSay(this._nudgeText);
1294:         if(this.nudges >= 2 && this._nudgeAnchor && this._nudgeAnchor.isConnected){
1295:           this.assisted = true;
1296:           Ghost.run(this, [this._nudgeAnchor]);
1297:         }
1298:       }
1299:     }, T(3000));
1300:     this._nudgeTimer = iv; this.timers.add(iv);
1301:   }
1302:   dispose(){
1303:     for(const id of this.timers){ clearTimeout(id); clearInterval(id); }
1304:     this.timers.clear();
1305:   }

 227: def assert_speech_rules(log, label):

def build():
    out = []
    for cid, desc in CHARS:
        out.append({"file": f"assets/char/{cid}.png", "prompt": f"{desc}. {STYLE_CHAR}", "transparent": True})
    for iid, desc in ITEMS:
        out.append({"file": f"assets/item/{iid}.png", "prompt": f"{desc}. {STYLE_ITEM}", "transparent": True})
    for iid, desc in ISLANDS:
        out.append({"file": f"assets/bg/{iid}.png", "prompt": f"{desc}. {STYLE_ISLAND}", "transparent": True})
    for bid, desc in BGS:
        out.append({"file": f"assets/bg/{bid}.png", "prompt": f"{desc}. {STYLE_BG}", "transparent": False, "size": "1536x1024"})
    return out

if __name__ == "__main__":
    import json
    m = build()
    print(json.dumps([a["file"] for a in m], indent=1))
    print(len(m), "assets")

self.addEventListener('install', e => {
  e.waitUntil((async () => {
    const c = await caches.open(CACHE);
    // æ ¸å¿ƒ shell åŽŸå­å®‰è£…: ä»»ä¸€å¤±è´¥ â†’ æ•´ä¸ª install å¤±è´¥, æ—§ç‰ˆæœ¬ç¼“å­˜ä¿æŒå¯ç”¨ï¼ˆA4ï¼‰
    await c.addAll(CORE);
    // å¯é€‰ç´ æé€ä¸ªç¼“å­˜, å•é¡¹å¤±è´¥ä¸é˜»å¡žï¼ˆè¿è¡Œæ—¶ç¼“å­˜è¡¥é½ï¼‰
    await Promise.allSettled(ASSETS.map(u => c.add(u)));
    await self.skipWaiting();
  })());
});

self.addEventListener('activate', e => {
  e.waitUntil((async () => {
    const keys = await caches.keys();
    await Promise.all(keys.filter(k => k !== CACHE).map(k => caches.delete(k)));
    await self.clients.claim();
  })());
});

self.addEventListener('fetch', e => {
  const req = e.request;
  if (req.method !== 'GET') return;
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  if (url.origin !== location.origin) return;
  const isPage = req.mode === 'navigate' || url.pathname.endsWith('/index.html') || url.pathname.endsWith('/');
  if (isPage) {
    // ç½‘ç»œä¼˜å…ˆï¼šä¿® bug åŽç”¨æˆ·åˆ·æ–°å³å¾—æ–°ç‰ˆï¼›æ–­ç½‘å›žé€€ç¼“å­˜
    e.respondWith((async () => {
      try {
        const r = await fetch(req);
        if (r && r.ok) {
          const copy = r.clone();
          e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE).then(c => c.put(req, copy)));  // å›žå¡«åœ¨ worker ç”Ÿå‘½å‘¨æœŸå†…å®Œæˆ
        }
        return r;
      } catch (err) {
        const c = await caches.open(CACHE);
        return (await c.match(req)) || (await c.match('./')) || (await c.match('index.html')) || Response.error();
      }
    })());
    return;
  }
  // ç´ æç¼“å­˜ä¼˜å…ˆï¼Œæœªå‘½ä¸­å›žæºå¹¶å›žå¡«
  e.respondWith((async () => {
    const hit = await caches.match(req);
    if (hit) return hit;
    try {
      const r = await fetch(req);
      if (r && r.ok) {
        const copy = r.clone();
        e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE).then(c => c.put(req, copy)));
      }
      return r;
    } catch (err) { return Response.error(); }
  })());
});

  16:     issues = []
  17:     if not os.path.exists(p):
  18:         return ["MISSING"]
  19:     im = Image.open(p); im.load()
  20:     w, h = im.size
  21:     if rel.startswith("assets/bg/bg_"):
  22:         if w < 1000: issues.append(f"small {w}x{h}")
  23:     elif max(w, h) < 500:
  24:         issues.append(f"small {w}x{h}")
  25:     if asset["transparent"]:
  26:         rgba = im.convert("RGBA")
  27:         px = rgba.load()
  28:         a = rgba.split()[3]
  29:         for xy in [(2,2),(w-3,2),(2,h-3),(w-3,h-3)]:
  30:             if a.getpixel(xy) > 12: issues.append(f"corner opaque {xy}"); break
  31:         bbox = a.point(lambda v: 255 if v > 16 else 0).getbbox()
  32:         if not bbox:
  33:             issues.append("empty")
  34:         else:
  35:             cov = (bbox[2]-bbox[0])*(bbox[3]-bbox[1])/(w*h)
  36:             if cov < 0.22: issues.append(f"subject tiny cov={cov:.2f}")
  37:             if cov > 0.97: issues.append(f"likely cropped cov={cov:.2f}")
  38:             if bbox[0] <= 1 or bbox[1] <= 1 or bbox[2] >= w-1 or bbox[3] >= h-1:

  41:             semi = bright = 0
  42:             step = max(1, (bbox[2]-bbox[0]) // 64)

  44:                 for x in range(bbox[0], bbox[2], step):

  55: def contact_sheet(files, out, cols=6, cell=200, label=True):
  56:     from PIL import ImageDraw
  57:     rows = (len(files) + cols - 1) // cols
  58:     sheet = Image.new("RGB", (cols*cell, rows*(cell+18)), (245, 245, 250))
  59:     d = ImageDraw.Draw(sheet)
  60:     for i, rel in enumerate(files):
  61:         p = os.path.join(ROOT, rel)
  62:         if not os.path.exists(p): continue
  63:         im = Image.open(p).convert("RGBA")
  64:         im.thumbnail((cell-8, cell-8), Image.LANCZOS)
  65:         x = (i % cols)*cell; y = (i // cols)*(cell+18)
  66:         bg = Image.new("RGBA", (cell, cell), (255,255,255,255))
  67:         bg.paste(im, ((cell-im.size[0])//2, (cell-im.size[1])//2), im)
  68:         sheet.paste(bg.convert("RGB"), (x, y))
  69:         if label:
  70:             d.text((x+6, y+cell+2), os.path.basename(rel).replace(".png",""), fill=(60,60,80))
  71:     sheet.save(os.path.join(ROOT, out), optimize=True)
  72:     print("sheet:", out)

"""Regenerate sw.js precache list from files on disk (run after asset changes)."""
import os, io

ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))
CACHE_VER = "km-v1"

def main():
    core = ["./", "index.html", "manifest.webmanifest",
            "assets/icons/icon-192.png", "assets/icons/icon-512.png", "assets/icons/apple-touch-icon.png"]
    assets = []
    for sub in ["assets/char", "assets/item", "assets/bg"]:
        d = os.path.join(ROOT, sub)
        if not os.path.isdir(d):
            continue
        for f in sorted(os.listdir(d)):
            if f.lower().endswith(".png"):
                assets.append(f"{sub}/{f}")
    core_lst = ",\n  ".join(f"'{a}'" for a in core)

    await self.skipWaiting();
  })());
});

  e.waitUntil((async () => {
    const keys = await caches.keys();
    await Promise.all(keys.filter(k => k !== CACHE).map(k => caches.delete(k)));
    await self.clients.claim();
  })());
});

self.addEventListener('fetch', e => {
  const req = e.request;
  if (req.method !== 'GET') return;
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  if (url.origin !== location.origin) return;
  const isPage = req.mode === 'navigate' || url.pathname.endsWith('/index.html') || url.pathname.endsWith('/');
  if (isPage) {

    e.respondWith((async () => {
      try {
        const r = await fetch(req);
        if (r && r.ok) {
          const copy = r.clone();
          e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE).then(c => c.put(req, copy)));  // å›žå¡«åœ¨ worker ç”Ÿå‘½å‘¨æœŸå†…å®Œæˆ
        }
        return r;
      } catch (err) {
        const c = await caches.open(CACHE);

    })());
    return;

  e.respondWith((async () => {
    const hit = await caches.match(req);
    if (hit) return hit;
    try {
      const r = await fetch(req);
      if (r && r.ok) {
        const copy = r.clone();
        e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE).then(c => c.put(req, copy)));
      }
      return r;
    } catch (err) { return Response.error(); }
  })());
});

## æ®‹ä½™é™åˆ¶è¯„ä¼°

- **æ¯”è¾ƒæ´»åŠ¨çº¯è§†è§‰è¡¨è¾¾ï¼šä¸æŽ¥å—ã€‚** åŽæœŸè§£é”å’Œé‡å¬æŒ‰é’®éƒ½ä¸èƒ½å¸®åŠ©æ— å£°çŠ¶æ€ï¼›é”™è¯¯ç¤ºèŒƒä¹Ÿæ²¡æœ‰è§†è§‰èƒœè€…æ ‡è¯†ã€‚è¿™æ˜¯ P0-03 çš„æ ¸å¿ƒéœ€æ±‚ï¼Œä¸æ˜¯å¯ç•™åˆ°äº¤ä»˜é˜¶æ®µçš„è£…é¥°é¡¹ã€‚

## æ®‹ä½™é™åˆ¶è¯„ä¼°

- **æ¯”è¾ƒæ´»åŠ¨çº¯è§†è§‰è¡¨è¾¾ï¼šä¸æŽ¥å—ã€‚** åŽæœŸè§£é”å’Œé‡å¬æŒ‰é’®éƒ½ä¸èƒ½å¸®åŠ©æ— å£°çŠ¶æ€ï¼›é”™è¯¯ç¤ºèŒƒä¹Ÿæ²¡æœ‰è§†è§‰èƒœè€…æ ‡è¯†ã€‚è¿™æ˜¯ P0-03 çš„æ ¸å¿ƒéœ€æ±‚ï¼Œä¸æ˜¯å¯ç•™åˆ°äº¤ä»˜é˜¶æ®µçš„è£…é¥°é¡¹ã€‚

- **è´´çº¸ç™½æè¾¹ï¼šæŽ¥å—ã€‚** ä¹é¡¹äº®è¾¹æç¤ºä¸Žé£Žæ ¼ä¸²ä¸­çš„ sticker æè¾¹ä¸€è‡´ï¼Œç›®æ£€æ²¡æœ‰æŠ å›¾è„è¾¹ã€‚

+def sha12(path):
+    h = hashlib.sha1()
+    with open(path, "rb") as f:
+        h.update(f.read())
+    return h.hexdigest()[:12]

 def contact_sheet(files, out, cols=6, cell=200, label=True):
     from PIL import ImageDraw
     rows = (len(files) + cols - 1) // cols
     sheet = Image.new("RGB", (cols*cell, rows*(cell+18)), (245, 245, 250))
     d = ImageDraw.Draw(sheet)
     for i, rel in enumerate(files):
         p = os.path.join(ROOT, rel)
         if not os.path.exists(p): continue
         im = Image.open(p).convert("RGBA")
         im.thumbnail((cell-8, cell-8), Image.LANCZOS)
         x = (i % cols)*cell; y = (i // cols)*(cell+18)
         bg = Image.new("RGBA", (cell, cell), (255,255,255,255))
         bg.paste(im, ((cell-im.size[0])//2, (cell-im.size[1])//2), im)
         sheet.paste(bg.convert("RGB"), (x, y))
         if label:
             d.text((x+6, y+cell+2), os.path.basename(rel).replace(".png",""), fill=(60,60,80))
     sheet.save(os.path.join(ROOT, out), optimize=True)
     print("sheet:", out)

  648: const Skills = {
  649:   lvl(k){ const d = Store.data; return d.diff === 'auto' ? d.skills[k].lvl : parseInt(d.diff, 10); },
  650:   record(k, firstTry){
  651:     const s = Store.data.skills[k]; if(!s) return;
  652:     s.tries++;
  653:     if(firstTry){ s.wins++; s.up++; s.down = 0; if(s.up >= 3 && s.lvl < 3){ s.lvl++; s.up = 0; } }

  656:   },
  657:   unlockedTypes(){
  658:     const sk = Store.data.skills, t = ['count','numeral'];

  660:     if(sk.count.lvl >= 2){ t.push('compare','compose'); }

  663:   },
  664:   plan(){

  666:     const plan = ['count'];
  667:     let pool = avail.slice();

  669:       let cand = pool.filter(t => t !== plan[plan.length - 1]);
  670:       if(!cand.length) cand = pool;
  671:       cand.sort((a,b) => (sk[a].wins + rng()) - (sk[b].wins + rng()));
  672:       plan.push(cand[0]);
  673:     }
  674:     if(avail.includes('numeral') && !plan.includes('numeral')) plan[1 + ri(4)] = 'numeral';
  675:     return plan;
  676:   },
  677:   gen(type){
  678:     // é‡å¤è§„é¿: ä¸Žä¸Šä¸€é¢˜å‚æ•°å®Œå…¨ç›¸åŒæ—¶é‡æŽ·ä¸€æ¬¡ï¼ˆT7ï¼‰
  679:     let p = this._gen1(type);
  680:     this._last = this._last || {};
  681:     if(this._last[type] === JSON.stringify(p)) p = this._gen1(type);
  682:     this._last[type] = JSON.stringify(p);
  683:     return p;
  684:   },
  685:   _gen1(type){
  686:     const L = this.lvl(type);
  687:     switch(type){
  688:       case 'count':  return {n: L === 1 ? 1 + ri(3) : L === 2 ? 2 + ri(4) : 4 + ri(7)};
  689:       case 'numeral':{
  690:         const range = L === 1 ? 3 : L === 2 ? 5 : 10, cnt = L === 3 ? 4 : 3;
  691:         return {target: 1 + ri(range), range, cnt};
  692:       }
  693:       case 'produce':return {n: L === 1 ? 2 + ri(2) : L === 2 ? 2 + ri(4) : 3 + ri(4)};
  694:       case 'flash':  return {n: L === 1 ? 1 + ri(3) : 1 + ri(4)};   // æ„Ÿæ•°: 1-4, ä¸éœ€é€ä¸€ç‚¹æ•°
  695:       case 'compare':{
  696:         const diff = L === 1 ? 3 : L === 2 ? 2 : 1;
  697:         const small = 1 + ri(3), big = Math.min(small + diff, 6);
  698:         return {a: big, b: small};
  699:       }
  700:       case 'compose':{
  701:         // L3 æ€»æ•°â‰¤8 ä¸”ä¸¤ç»„å„â‰¤5: 88px æºç‰©å“ç«–å±ä¸¤åˆ—å¸ƒå±€çš„å¯å®¹çº³ä¸Šç•Œï¼ˆP1-04ï¼‰
  702:         const total = L === 1 ? 3 + ri(2) : L === 2 ? 5 : 6 + ri(3);
  703:         const lo = Math.max(1, total - 5), hi = Math.min(5, total - 1);
  704:         const a = lo + ri(hi - lo + 1);
  705:         return {a, b: total - a};
  706:       }
  707:       case 'add':{
  708:         const total = L === 1 ? 2 + ri(3) : L === 2 ? 3 + ri(3) : 6 + ri(5);
  709:         let a = 1 + ri(Math.min(5, total - 1)); let b = total - a;
  710:         if(b > 5){ b = 5; a = total - b; }
  711:         return {a, b};
  712:       }
  713:     }
  714:   }
  715: };

  752: function scatterPositions(n){
  753:   const cols = n <= 3 ? n : n <= 6 ? 3 : n <= 8 ? 4 : 4;
  754:   const rows = Math.ceil(n / cols);
  755:   const cells = [];

  335: /* ============ 4. Speech（iOS 军规引擎） ============ */
  336: const Speech = {
  337:   noTTS: !('speechSynthesis' in window) || !('SpeechSynthesisUtterance' in window),
  338:   unlocked:false, everWorked:false, voice:null, voices:[],
  339:   lastReq:0, welcomeText:'', welcomeAt:0, _respoke:false,
  340:   _refs:[], _pend:null, _cancelT:null, _gen:0,
  341:   _countQ:[], _latestCount:null,
  342:   log:[],

  344:   init(){
  345:     if(this.noTTS) return;
  346:     const load = () => { try{ this.voices = speechSynthesis.getVoices() || []; }catch(e){ this.voices=[]; } this.pickVoice(); };
  347:     load();
  348:     try{
  349:       speechSynthesis.onvoiceschanged = () => {
  350:         const hadZh = !!this.voice;
  351:         load();
  352:         // 军规6: 声音列表晚到 → 用正确声音补说第一句（严格限定条件, 见 DESIGN v2）
  353:         if(this.unlocked && !this.everWorked && !this._respoke && this.voice && !hadZh
  354:            && Date.now() - this.welcomeAt < 10000 && this.welcomeText){
  355:           this._respoke = true;
  356:           const gen = this._gen;
  357:           setTimeout(() => {
  358:             if(gen !== this._gen) return;
  359:             if(speechSynthesis.speaking || speechSynthesis.pending) return;   // 军规7: 不插嘴
  360:             if(Date.now() - this.lastReq < 1500) return;

  362:           }, 300);
  363:         }
  364:       };
  365:     }catch(e){}
  366:     document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
  367:       if(!document.hidden){ try{ speechSynthesis.resume(); }catch(e){} }
  368:     });
  369:     window.addEventListener('pageshow', () => {
  370:       // bfcache 恢复: 全量清场（含底层引擎队列, 带180ms冷却窗）+ resume（§9.6）
  371:       this.stopAll();
  372:       try{ speechSynthesis.resume(); }catch(e){}
  373:     });
  374:   },

  376:   pickVoice(){
  377:     const stored = Store.data && Store.data.voiceURI;
  378:     const zh = this.voices.filter(v => /^zh/i.test(v.lang) || /中文|Chinese/i.test(v.name));
  379:     if(stored){ const m = this.voices.find(v => v.voiceURI === stored); if(m){ this.voice = m; return; } }
  380:     const score = v => (/zh[-_]CN/i.test(v.lang) ? 4 : 0)
  381:       + (/Tingting|Ting-Ting|婷婷/i.test(v.name) ? 3 : 0)
  382:       + (/Xiaoxiao|晓晓/i.test(v.name) ? 3 : 0)
  383:       + (v.localService ? 1 : 0) + (v.default ? .5 : 0);
  384:     this.voice = zh.sort((a,b) => score(b) - score(a))[0] || null;
  385:   },
  386: 
  387:   unlock(text){
  388:     // 军规1: 必须在 click 抬起手势的同步调用栈内首次 speak
  389:     this.unlocked = true; this.welcomeText = text; this.welcomeAt = Date.now();
  390:     this.say(text);
  391:     setTimeout(() => { if(!this.everWorked) UI.showNoSound(); }, 3500);
  392:   },
  393: 
  394:   /* 指令/反馈通道: 最新覆盖 + 安全打断(cancel 后 ≥180ms) */
  395:   say(text, opts){
  396:     opts = opts || {};
  397:     if(this.noTTS || !this.unlocked || !text) return Promise.resolve();
  398:     if(opts.polite){
  399:       try{ if(speechSynthesis.speaking || speechSynthesis.pending) return Promise.resolve(); }catch(e){}
  400:       if(Date.now() - this.lastReq < 1500) return Promise.resolve();
  401:     }
  402:     this.lastReq = Date.now();
  403:     try{ speechSynthesis.resume(); }catch(e){}   // 军规4
  404:     let busy = false;
  405:     try{ busy = speechSynthesis.speaking || speechSynthesis.pending; }catch(e){}
  406:     if(busy || this._cancelT){
  407:       return new Promise(res => {
  408:         if(this._pend) this._pend.res();          // 军规8: 旧的被最新覆盖，等待者立即放行
  409:         this._pend = {text, opts, res};
  410:         if(!this._cancelT){
  411:           try{ speechSynthesis.cancel(); }catch(e){}
  412:           this._instrActive = false;
  413:           this._countQ.length = 0; this._latestCount = null;
  414:           this._cancelT = setTimeout(() => {      // 军规2: cancel 后 ≥150ms
  415:             this._cancelT = null;
  416:             const p = this._pend; this._pend = null;
  417:             if(p) this._speakNow(p.text, p.opts, p.res);
  418:           }, 180);
  419:         }
  420:       });
  421:     }
  422:     return new Promise(res => this._speakNow(text, opts, res));
  423:   },
  424: 
  425:   politeSay(text){ return this.say(text, {polite:true}); },
  426: 
  427:   _mkUtter(text, opts){
  428:     const u = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
  429:     if(this.voice) u.voice = this.voice;
  430:     u.lang = 'zh-CN';                              // 恒设 lang（无 voice 时兜底）
  431:     u.rate = opts.rate || .92; u.pitch = opts.pitch || 1.1; u.volume = 1;
  432:     this._refs.push(u);                            // 军规6: 防 GC
  433:     return u;
  434:   },
  435:   _release(u){ const i = this._refs.indexOf(u); if(i >= 0) this._refs.splice(i, 1); },
  436:   _markWork(){ this.everWorked = true; UI.hideNoSound(); },
  437: 
  438:   _speakNow(text, opts, res){
  439:     let u;
  440:     try{ u = this._mkUtter(text, opts); }catch(e){ res(); return; }
  441:     this.log.push({t:Date.now(), text});
  442:     const gen = this._gen;
  443:     const uid = ++this._uidSeq || (this._uidSeq = 1);
  444:     let settled = false, started = false;
  445:     this._instrActive = !opts.isCount;
  446:     if(!opts.isCount) this._instrUid = uid;        // 当前指令话语标识
  447:     if(opts.isCount) this._activeCountSay = (this._activeCountSay || 0) + 1;  // drain 追踪 say 通道数词（P1-01）
  448:     if(!opts.isCount) Sfx.duck(true);              // 语音期间音效闪避（U7）
  449:     // 旧回调退休契约（§9.6/P1-02）: 事件与看门狗只在"同代际且仍是当前话语"时才允许改全局状态;
  450:     // 过期回调只清理自身引用并放行 Promise, 不得污染新语境
  451:     const finish = () => { if(!settled){ settled = true;
  452:       const fresh = gen === this._gen;
  453:       if(!opts.isCount){ if(fresh && this._instrUid === uid){ this._instrActive = false; Sfx.duck(false); } }
  454:       else if(fresh){ this._activeCountSay = Math.max(0, (this._activeCountSay || 1) - 1); }
  455:       this._release(u); res(); } };
  456:     u.onstart = () => { started = true; this._markWork(); };
  457:     u.onboundary = () => { started = true; this._markWork(); };
  458:     u.onend = () => { this._markWork(); finish(); };
  459:     u.onerror = e => { if(e && e.error === 'interrupted'){} finish(); };
  460:     // 军规3: onend 不可信 → 超时兜底
  461:     const tmo = opts.tmo || Math.max(2500, text.length * 380);
  462:     setTimeout(finish, tmo);
  463:     try{ speechSynthesis.resume(); speechSynthesis.speak(u); }catch(e){ finish(); return; }
  464:     // 健康信号轮询（250/450ms）
  465:     [250, 450].forEach(d => setTimeout(() => {
  466:       try{ if(speechSynthesis.speaking || speechSynthesis.pending) this._markWork(); }catch(e){}
  467:     }, d));
  468:     // 被吞恢复: 700ms 无事件且引擎全空闲 → 同文本重试一次（generation token 保护）
  469:     setTimeout(() => {
  470:       if(settled || started || gen !== this._gen || this._cancelT) return;
  471:       let idle = false;
  472:       try{ idle = !speechSynthesis.speaking && !speechSynthesis.pending; }catch(e){}
  473:       if(idle){
  474:         try{
  475:           const u2 = this._mkUtter(text, opts);
  476:           u2.onend = () => { this._markWork(); finish(); };
  477:           u2.onerror = () => finish();
  478:           u2.onstart = () => this._markWork();
  479:           speechSynthesis.speak(u2);
  480:           setTimeout(() => { this._release(u2); }, tmo);
  481:         }catch(e){}
  482:       }
  483:     }, 700);
  484:   },
  485: 
  486:   /* 计数词通道: 有序自然队列（不 cancel, 保听觉一一对应）; 积压>2 只留最新（军规8收敛）。
  487:      若指令语音正在播（孩子等不及开始点数）→ 首个数词走安全打断通道抢占指令 */
  488:   countSay(word){
  489:     if(this.noTTS || !this.unlocked) return;
  490:     if(this._instrActive || this._cancelT){
  491:       this.say(word, {rate:1.05, pitch:1.15, tmo:1600, isCount:true});
  492:       return;
  493:     }
  494:     this.lastReq = Date.now();
  495:     try{ speechSynthesis.resume(); }catch(e){}
  496:     this._countQ = this._countQ.filter(q => !q.done);
  497:     if(this._countQ.length >= 3) return;           // 防御性硬上限（正常被 countBusy 背压挡住, 军规8: 绝不排长队）
  498:     this._enqCount(word);
  499:   },
  500:   /* 输入背压: 计数词积压≥2 时活动应暂缓接受新的计数点击（保证听觉序列完整不丢词） */
  501:   countBusy(){
  502:     if(this.noTTS || !this.unlocked) return false;
  503:     this._countQ = this._countQ.filter(q => !q.done);
  504:     if(this._countQ.length >= 2) return true;
  505:     if(this._cancelT && this._pend && this._pend.opts && this._pend.opts.isCount) return true;
  506:     return false;
  507:   },
  508:   /* 计数队列排空（P1-01）: 末数播完(或有界看门狗到期)再进入提问。
  509:      覆盖三处在途数词: 自然队列 _countQ / cancel 等待窗内的 pending count / say 通道在播的 isCount */
  510:   drain(maxMs){
  511:     const deadline = Date.now() + (maxMs || 4000);
  512:     return new Promise(res => {
  513:       const chk = () => {
  514:         this._countQ = this._countQ.filter(q => !q.done);
  515:         const busyCount = this._countQ.length > 0
  516:           || (this._cancelT && this._pend && this._pend.opts && this._pend.opts.isCount)
  517:           || (this._activeCountSay || 0) > 0;
  518:         if(!busyCount || Date.now() > deadline) res();
  519:         else setTimeout(chk, 120);
  520:       };
  521:       chk();
  522:     });
  523:   },
  524:   _enqCount(word){
  525:     let u;
  526:     try{ u = this._mkUtter(word, {rate:1.0, pitch:1.15}); }catch(e){ return; }
  527:     this.log.push({t:Date.now(), text:word, count:true});
  528:     const gen = this._gen;
  529:     const q = {u, done:false};
  530:     const fin = () => {
  531:       if(q.done) return;
  532:       q.done = true; this._release(u);
  533:       if(gen !== this._gen) return;               // 过期数词回调: 只自清理, 不动新语境队列（P1-02）
  534:       this._countQ = this._countQ.filter(x => x !== q);
  535:       if(this._latestCount && this._countQ.length <= 1){
  536:         const w = this._latestCount; this._latestCount = null; this._enqCount(w);
  537:       }
  538:     };
  539:     u.onstart = () => this._markWork();
  540:     u.onend = () => { this._markWork(); fin(); };
  541:     u.onerror = fin;
  542:     // 引擎从未出声(疑似静音/不可用)时用短看门狗, 避免背压把游戏节奏卡成 1.6s/次
  543:     setTimeout(fin, this.everWorked ? 1600 : 500);
  544:     this._countQ.push(q);
  545:     try{ speechSynthesis.speak(u); }catch(e){ fin(); }
  546:   },
  547: 
  548:   stopAll(){
  549:     this._gen++;
  550:     this._instrActive = false;
  551:     this._activeCountSay = 0;
  552:     Sfx.duck(false);
  553:     if(this._pend){ this._pend.res(); this._pend = null; }
  554:     if(this._cancelT){ clearTimeout(this._cancelT); this._cancelT = null; }
  555:     this._countQ.length = 0; this._latestCount = null;

  29: def sha12(path):
  30:     h = hashlib.sha1()
  31:     with open(path, "rb") as f:
  32:         h.update(f.read())
  33:     return h.hexdigest()[:12]

  35: def qa(asset):
  36:     """自动检查: 尺寸 / 四角alpha / 主体bbox占比(22%-97%, 与DESIGN §9.5一致) /
  37:     贴边裁切 / 边缘halo(半透明亮色描边采样)。"""
  38:     rel = asset["file"]; p = os.path.join(ROOT, rel)
  39:     issues = []
  40:     if not os.path.exists(p):
  41:         return ["MISSING"]
  42:     im = Image.open(p); im.load()
  43:     w, h = im.size
  44:     if rel.startswith("assets/bg/bg_"):
  45:         if w < 1000: issues.append(f"small {w}x{h}")
  46:     elif max(w, h) < 500:
  47:         issues.append(f"small {w}x{h}")
  48:     if asset["transparent"]:
  49:         rgba = im.convert("RGBA")
  50:         px = rgba.load()
  51:         a = rgba.split()[3]
  52:         for xy in [(2,2),(w-3,2),(2,h-3),(w-3,h-3)]:
  53:             if a.getpixel(xy) > 12: issues.append(f"corner opaque {xy}"); break
  54:         bbox = a.point(lambda v: 255 if v > 16 else 0).getbbox()
  55:         if not bbox:
  56:             issues.append("empty")
  57:         else:
  58:             cov = (bbox[2]-bbox[0])*(bbox[3]-bbox[1])/(w*h)
  59:             if cov < 0.22: issues.append(f"subject tiny cov={cov:.2f}")
  60:             if cov > 0.97: issues.append(f"likely cropped cov={cov:.2f}")
  61:             if bbox[0] <= 1 or bbox[1] <= 1 or bbox[2] >= w-1 or bbox[3] >= h-1:
  62:                 issues.append("touches edge (possible crop)")
  63:             # 边缘 halo: 只扫 bbox 外圈环带(宽度=bbox短边8%), 半透明亮白像素占比过高 = 疑似抠图脏边
  64:             bw, bh = bbox[2]-bbox[0], bbox[3]-bbox[1]
  65:             ring = max(2, min(bw, bh) // 12)
  66:             semi = bright = 0
  67:             step = max(1, bw // 96)
  68:             for y in range(bbox[1], bbox[3], step):
  69:                 for x in range(bbox[0], bbox[2], step):

  71:                     if not in_ring:

  76:                         if r > 235 and g > 235 and b > 235: bright += 1

  - count/add/compose 会从下一未处理物品切换到答案牌区。[index.html:902](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:902) [index.html:1220](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:1220) [index.html:1280](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:1280)
  - numeral 锁定正确且未爆气球；produce 在喂够后切换到绿勾。[index.html:985](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:985) [index.html:1045](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:1045)
  - flash 指向牌区；compare 使用稳定的获胜侧元素。compare 虽是直接元素而非函数，但目标不会随阶段变化，因此不存在随机或失效指向。[index.html:953](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:953) [index.html:1162](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:1162)

- P1-02：`_speakNow()` 已按 generation 与当前 instruction UID 双闸保护 `_instrActive` 和 duck；计数通道按 generation 保护 `_activeCountSay`。[index.html:438](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:438) `_enqCount()` 也在触碰新队列前退休过期回调。[index.html:524](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:524) `stopAll()` 会推进 generation 并重置全局状态。[index.html:548](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:548)
